Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the neuro framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 145. The resulting operator achieves bound O(499/n^2).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by neuro generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 145, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 499. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the neuro object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 468. The moduli space has dimension at most 339 with 4 connected components.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on neuro structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 468. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 339. QED.
